# Mantine Core

> Generated from `component-catalog-v1.json`. Catalog schema v1 · Custom Widget 2.0.0 · Mantine 9.4.1. Do not edit this file by hand.

Package: `@mantine/core`. Effective props combine the [shared prop API](components.md#shared-prop-api) with each component's specific props below. Component-specific records override shared records with the same name.

## Package guidance

Use declarative props and `bind` adapters instead of event callbacks. Homarr scopes supported overlays to the widget root. Use Homarr's `RecursiveList` when a tree needs a custom arbitrary-depth node template.

<a id="component-accordion"></a>
### `Accordion`

- Safety: `wrapped`
- Category: `navigation`
- Documentation: [upstream component API](https://mantine.dev/core/accordion/)
- Temporary bind: `string` via `defaultValue`; read from `inputs.<bindName>`; becomes `string[]` when `multiple` is true
- Subcomponents: <code>Accordion.Chevron</code>, <code>Accordion.Control</code>, <code>Accordion.Item</code>, <code>Accordion.Panel</code>
- Component-specific blocked props: none
- Accessibility:
  - Provide an accessible label for controls without visible text.

#### Component-specific props

| Prop | Required | Type | Literal values | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>attributes</code> | no | <code>{ label?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; content?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; root?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; item?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; ... 4 more ...; control?: Record&lt;...&gt;; }</code> | — | — |
| <code>chevron</code> | no | <code>React.ReactNode</code> | — | Custom chevron icon |
| <code>chevronIconSize</code> | no | <code>string &#124; number</code> | — | Size of the default chevron icon. Ignored when `chevron` prop is set. Use `chevronSize` instead when using custom chevron. |
| <code>chevronPosition</code> | no | <code>"left" &#124; "right"</code> | <code>"left"</code>, <code>"right"</code> | Position of the chevron relative to the item label |
| <code>chevronSize</code> | no | <code>string &#124; number</code> | — | Size of the chevron icon container |
| <code>disableChevronRotation</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, chevron rotation is disabled |
| <code>keepMounted</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set to `false`, panels are unmounted when collapsed. By default, panels stay mounted when collapsed. |
| <code>loop</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, arrow keys loop through items (first to last and last to first) |
| <code>transitionDuration</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Transition duration in ms |
| <code>unstyled</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | — |

| Prop | Required | Type | Literal values | Description |

| <code>accentInsensitive</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Perform accent-insensitive matching. When enabled cafe will match cafe, café, cafè, etc. |
| <code>attributes</code> | no | <code>{ root?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; }</code> | — | — |
| <code>caseInsensitive</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Perform case-insensitive matching. |
| <code>gradient</code> | no | <code>MantineGradient</code> | — | Gradient configuration, ignored when `variant` is not `gradient` |
| <code>highlight</code> | yes | <code>string &#124; string[] &#124; HighlightTerm[]</code> | — | Substring(s) to highlight in `children`. Can be: - string: single term - string[]: multiple terms with same color - HighlightTerm[]: multiple terms with custom colors per term - Matching is case-insensitive and accent-insensitive by default, use `caseInsensitive` and `accentInsensitive` props to control this behavior - Regex special characters are automatically escaped - When multiple substrings are provided, longer matches take precedence - Empty strings and whitespace-only strings are ignored |
| <code>inherit</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether font properties should be inherited from the parent |
| <code>inline</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Sets `line-height` to 1 for centering |
| <code>lineClamp</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Number of lines after which Text will be truncated |
| <code>span</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Shorthand for `component="span"` |
| <code>textWrap</code> | no | <code>"wrap" &#124; "nowrap" &#124; "balance" &#124; "pretty" &#124; "stable"</code> | <code>"balance"</code>, <code>"nowrap"</code>, <code>"pretty"</code>, <code>"stable"</code>, <code>"wrap"</code> | Controls `text-wrap` CSS property |
| <code>truncate</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true &#124; "end" &#124; "start"</code> | <code>"end"</code>, <code>false</code>, <code>"start"</code>, <code>true</code> | Side on which Text must be truncated, if `true`, text is truncated from the start |
| <code>unstyled</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | — |
| <code>wholeWord</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Only match whole words (adds word boundaries to regex). When enabled, 'the' will not match 'there'. |

| <code>hideDetached</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is hidden when the element is hidden with styles or not visible on the screen |
| <code>initiallyOpened</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Initial opened state |
| <code>keepMounted</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is not unmounted from the DOM when hidden. `display: none` styles are added instead. |

| <code>preventPositionChangeWhenVisible</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If `true`, the dropdown picks its side on open (flip runs once, preferring the `position` prop) and then never changes side — scrolling, resizing, and content size changes will not flip the dropdown. The side is recalculated fresh on the next open. Does not affect the `shift` middleware. Set to `false` to keep flip active and allow the dropdown to re-flip on every change. |

| <code>arrowPosition</code> | no | <code>"center" &#124; "side" &#124; "merge"</code> | <code>"center"</code>, <code>"merge"</code>, <code>"side"</code> | Arrow position |
| <code>arrowRadius</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Arrow `border-radius` in px |
| <code>arrowSize</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Arrow size in px |
| <code>attributes</code> | no | <code>{ label?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; search?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; item?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; chevron?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; ... 6 more ...; divider?: Record&lt;...&gt;; }</code> | — | — |
| <code>checkIcon</code> | no | <code>React.ReactNode</code> | — | Custom icon rendered as the indicator of checked `Menu.CheckboxItem` and selected `Menu.RadioItem`. Can be overridden per item with the `checkIcon` prop. |
| <code>clickOutsideEvents</code> | no | <code>string[]</code> | — | Events that trigger outside click detection. Includes mousedown for desktop clicks, touchstart for mobile, and keydown for Escape key handling |
| <code>closeDelay</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Close delay in ms, applicable only to `trigger="hover"` variant |
| <code>closeOnClickOutside</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is closed on outside clicks |
| <code>closeOnEscape</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is closed when the `Escape` key is pressed |
| <code>closeOnItemClick</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the Menu is closed when one of the items is clicked. Can be overridden per item with `closeMenuOnClick` prop |
| <code>defaultOpened</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Uncontrolled menu initial opened state |
| <code>floatingStrategy</code> | no | <code>"fixed" &#124; "absolute"</code> | <code>"absolute"</code>, <code>"fixed"</code> | Changes floating ui [position strategy](https://floating-ui.com/docs/usefloating#strategy) |
| <code>hideDetached</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is hidden when the element is hidden with styles or not visible on the screen |
| <code>keepMounted</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is not unmounted from the DOM when hidden. `display: none` styles are added instead. |
| <code>keepMountedMode</code> | no | <code>"activity" &#124; "display-none"</code> | <code>"activity"</code>, <code>"display-none"</code> | Controls how the dropdown is hidden when `keepMounted` is set: `'activity'` – hidden with React 19 `Activity` component, `'display-none'` – hidden with `display: none` styles |
| <code>loop</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, arrow key presses wrap around from last item to first and vice versa |
| <code>menuItemTabIndex</code> | no | <code>0 &#124; -1</code> | <code>-1</code>, <code>0</code> | Set the `tabindex` on all menu items. Use `0` to allow Tab key navigation through menu items (required for navigation menus following WAI-ARIA disclosure pattern). |

| <code>openDelay</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Open delay in ms, applicable only to `trigger="hover"` variant |
| <code>opened</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Controlled menu opened state |
| <code>overlayProps</code> | no | <code>(OverlayProps &amp; ElementProps&lt;"div"&gt;)</code> | — | Props passed down to `Overlay` component |
| <code>position</code> | no | <code>"left" &#124; "right" &#124; "bottom" &#124; "top" &#124; "left-end" &#124; "left-start" &#124; "right-end" &#124; "right-start" &#124; "bottom-end" &#124; "bottom-start" &#124; "top-end" &#124; "top-start"</code> | <code>"bottom"</code>, <code>"bottom-end"</code>, <code>"bottom-start"</code>, <code>"left"</code>, <code>"left-end"</code>, <code>"left-start"</code>, <code>"right"</code>, <code>"right-end"</code>, <code>"right-start"</code>, <code>"top"</code>, <code>"top-end"</code>, <code>"top-start"</code> | Dropdown position relative to the target element |
| <code>preventPositionChangeWhenVisible</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If `true`, the dropdown picks its side on open (flip runs once, preferring the `position` prop) and then never changes side — scrolling, resizing, and content size changes will not flip the dropdown. | <code>returnFocus</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether focus should be automatically returned to control when dropdown closes |

| <code>trapFocus</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, focus is trapped within the menu dropdown when it is opened |
| <code>trigger</code> | no | <code>"hover" &#124; "click" &#124; "click-hover"</code> | <code>"click"</code>, <code>"click-hover"</code>, <code>"hover"</code> | Event trigger to open menu. Note: 'hover' is not keyboard accessible; prefer 'click-hover' for accessible hover menus |

| <code>arrowPosition</code> | no | <code>"center" &#124; "side" &#124; "merge"</code> | <code>"center"</code>, <code>"merge"</code>, <code>"side"</code> | Arrow position |
| <code>arrowRadius</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Arrow `border-radius` in px |

| <code>closeDelay</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Close delay in ms, applicable when hover trigger is used |
| <code>floatingStrategy</code> | no | <code>"fixed" &#124; "absolute"</code> | <code>"absolute"</code>, <code>"fixed"</code> | Changes floating ui [position strategy](https://floating-ui.com/docs/usefloating#strategy) |
| <code>hideDetached</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is hidden when the element is hidden with styles or not visible on the screen |
| <code>keepMounted</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the dropdown is not unmounted from the DOM when hidden. `display: none` styles are added instead. |
| <code>keepMountedMode</code> | no | <code>"activity" &#124; "display-none"</code> | <code>"activity"</code>, <code>"display-none"</code> | Controls how the dropdown is hidden when `keepMounted` is set: `'activity'` – hidden with React 19 `Activity` component, `'display-none'` – hidden with `display: none` styles |
| <code>middlewares</code> | no | <code>PopoverMiddlewares</code> | — | Floating ui middlewares to configure position handling |
| <code>openDelay</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Open delay in ms, applicable when hover trigger is used |
| <code>opened</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Controlled opened state |
| <code>overlayProps</code> | no | <code>(OverlayProps &amp; ElementProps&lt;"div"&gt;)</code> | — | Props passed down to `Overlay` component |
| <code>position</code> | no | <code>"left" &#124; "right" &#124; "bottom" &#124; "top" &#124; "left-end" &#124; "left-start" &#124; "right-end" &#124; "right-start" &#124; "bottom-end" &#124; "bottom-start" &#124; "top-end" &#124; "top-start"</code> | <code>"bottom"</code>, <code>"bottom-end"</code>, <code>"bottom-start"</code>, <code>"left"</code>, <code>"left-end"</code>, <code>"left-start"</code>, <code>"right"</code>, <code>"right-end"</code>, <code>"right-start"</code>, <code>"top"</code>, <code>"top-end"</code>, <code>"top-start"</code> | Dropdown position relative to the target element |
| <code>preventPositionChangeWhenVisible</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If `true`, the dropdown picks its side on open (flip runs once, preferring the `position` prop) and then never changes side — scrolling, resizing, and content size changes will not flip the dropdown. The side is recalculated fresh on the next open. Does not affect the `shift` middleware. Set to `false` to keep flip active and allow the dropdown to re-flip on every change. |
| <code>returnFocus</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether focus should be automatically returned to control when dropdown closes |
| <code>safeAreaPolygon</code> | no | <code>boolean &#124; SafePolygonOptions</code> | — | Determines whether submenu stays open while the cursor moves toward its dropdown. Pass an object to configure safe polygon behavior. |
| <code>transitionProps</code> | no | <code>Partial&lt;Omit&lt;TransitionProps, "mounted"&gt;&gt;</code> | — | Props passed down to the `Transition` component that used to animate dropdown presence, use to configure duration and animation type |
| <code>withArrow</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether component should have an arrow |
| <code>withOverlay</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether the overlay should be displayed when the dropdown is opened |

| Prop | Required | Type | Literal values | Description |

| <code>attributes</code> | no | <code>{ label?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; root?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; description?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; error?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; success?: Record&lt;...&gt;; required?: Record&lt;...&gt;; }</code> | — | — |

| <code>descriptionProps</code> | no | <code>(InputDescriptionProps &amp; DataAttributes)</code> | — | Props passed down to the `Input.Description` component |
| <code>error</code> | no | <code>React.ReactNode</code> | — | Contents of `Input.Error` component. If not set, error is not displayed. |
| <code>errorProps</code> | no | <code>(InputErrorProps &amp; DataAttributes)</code> | — | Props passed down to the `Input.Error` component |
| <code>inputWrapperOrder</code> | no | <code>("input" &#124; "label" &#124; "description" &#124; "error")[]</code> | — | Controls order and visibility of wrapper elements. Only elements included in this array will be rendered. |

| <code>labelElement</code> | no | <code>"div" &#124; "label"</code> | <code>"div"</code>, <code>"label"</code> | Root element for the label. Use `'div'` when wrapper contains multiple input elements and you need to handle `htmlFor` manually. |

| <code>success</code> | no | <code>React.ReactNode</code> | — | Contents of `Input.Success` component. If not set, success is not displayed. |
| <code>successProps</code> | no | <code>(InputSuccessProps &amp; DataAttributes)</code> | — | Props passed down to the `Input.Success` component |
| <code>unstyled</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | — |
| <code>withAsterisk</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, the required asterisk is displayed next to the label. Overrides `required` prop. Does not add required attribute to the input. |
| <code>wrapperProps</code> | no | <code>(React.ClassAttributes&lt;HTMLDivElement&gt; &amp; React.HTMLAttributes&lt;HTMLDivElement&gt; &amp; DataAttributes)</code> | — | Props passed down to the `Input.Wrapper` |

### `RangeSlider`

- Safety: `wrapped`

- Documentation: [upstream component API](https://mantine.dev/core/range-slider/)
- Temporary bind: `number[]` via `defaultValue`; read from `inputs.<bindName>`

#### Component-specific props

| Prop | Required | Type | Literal values | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>attributes</code> | no | <code>{ label?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; mark?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; track?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; root?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; ... 4 more ...; markLabel?: Record&lt;...&gt;; }</code> | — | — |
| <code>domain</code> | no | <code>[number, number]</code> | — | Domain of the slider, defines the selectable value range independently of min/max. Useful when you want to display a wider track range (min/max) but restrict actual selection to a subset (domain). |
| <code>hiddenInputProps</code> | no | <code>React.DetailedHTMLProps&lt;React.InputHTMLAttributes&lt;HTMLInputElement&gt;, HTMLInputElement&gt;</code> | — | Props passed down to the hidden input |
| <code>inverted</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether track values representation should be inverted |
| <code>labelAlwaysOn</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether the label should be visible when the slider is not being dragged or hovered |
| <code>labelTransitionProps</code> | no | <code>Partial&lt;Omit&lt;TransitionProps, "mounted"&gt;&gt;</code> | — | Props passed down to the `Transition` component |
| <code>marks</code> | no | <code>SliderMark[]</code> | — | Marks displayed on the track |
| <code>maxRange</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Maximum range interval |
| <code>minRange</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Minimal range interval between the two thumbs. Consider this value relative to the total range (max - min). |
| <code>precision</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Number of significant digits after the decimal point |
| <code>pushOnOverlap</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether the other thumb should be pushed by the current thumb dragging when `minRange`/`maxRange` is reached |
| <code>restrictToMarks</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether the selection should be only allowed from the given marks array |
| <code>showLabelOnHover</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Determines whether the label should be displayed when the slider is hovered |
| <code>thumbChildren</code> | no | <code>React.ReactNode</code> | — | Content rendered inside thumb |
| <code>thumbFromLabel</code> | no | <code>string</code> | — | First thumb `aria-label` |
| <code>thumbLabel</code> | no | <code>string</code> | — | `aria-label` for both thumbs (overridden by thumbFromLabel/thumbToLabel if provided) |
| <code>thumbSize</code> | no | <code>string &#124; number</code> | — | Thumb `width` and `height`, by default value is computed based on `size` prop |
| <code>thumbToLabel</code> | no | <code>string</code> | — | Second thumb `aria-label` |
| <code>unstyled</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | — |

| Prop | Required | Type | Literal values | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>attributes</code> | no | <code>{ label?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; svg?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; root?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; curve?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; }</code> | — | — |
| <code>label</code> | no | <code>React.ReactNode</code> | — | Label displayed in the center of the ring |
| <code>rootColor</code> | no | <code>DefaultMantineColor</code> | <code>"blue"</code>, <code>"cyan"</code>, <code>"dark"</code>, <code>"grape"</code>, <code>"gray"</code>, <code>"green"</code>, <code>"indigo"</code>, <code>"lime"</code>, <code>"orange"</code>, <code>"pink"</code>, <code>"red"</code>, <code>"teal"</code>, <code>"violet"</code>, <code>"yellow"</code> | Color of the unfilled portion of the ring (background). Defaults to gray-2 in light mode, dark-4 in dark mode |
| <code>roundCaps</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Applies rounded line caps to the start and end of visible sections |

| <code>sections</code> | yes | <code>RingProgressSection[]</code> | — | Array of sections to display in the ring. Each section should have a `value` (0-100), `color`, and optional `tooltip`. Sections can also receive any valid SVG circle element props. |
| <code>startAngle</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Starting angle in degrees. 0 = right, 90 = bottom, 180 = left, 270 = top |
| <code>thickness</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Ring thickness in pixels. Cannot exceed size / 4 and will be automatically clamped if necessary |
| <code>transitionDuration</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Transition duration in milliseconds for section value and color changes |
| <code>unstyled</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | — |

<a id="component-scrollarea"></a>
### `ScrollArea`

- Safety: `wrapped`
- Category: `layout`
- Documentation: [upstream component API](https://mantine.dev/core/scroll-area/)
- Temporary bind: not available
- Subcomponents: <code>ScrollArea.Autosize</code>
- Component-specific blocked props: none
- Accessibility:
  - Keep visual order consistent with reading order.

#### Component-specific props

| Prop | Required | Type | Literal values | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>attributes</code> | no | <code>{ content?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; root?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; scrollbar?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; thumb?: Record&lt;string, any&gt;; viewport?: Record&lt;...&gt;; corner?: Record&lt;...&gt;; }</code> | — | — |
| <code>offsetScrollbars</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true &#124; "x" &#124; "y" &#124; "present"</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>"present"</code>, <code>true</code>, <code>"x"</code>, <code>"y"</code> | Determines whether scrollbars should be offset with padding on given axis - `true` - adds padding to offset both scrollbars (always) - `'x'` - adds padding to offset horizontal scrollbar (always) - `'y'` - adds padding to offset vertical scrollbar (always) - `'present'` - adds padding only when scrollbars are visible (dynamic) |
| <code>overscrollBehavior</code> | no | <code>Property.OverscrollBehavior</code> | <code>"-moz-initial"</code>, <code>"auto"</code>, <code>"contain"</code>, <code>"inherit"</code>, <code>"initial"</code>, <code>"none"</code>, <code>"revert"</code>, <code>"revert-layer"</code>, <code>"unset"</code> | Defines `overscroll-behavior` of the viewport |
| <code>scrollHideDelay</code> | no | <code>number</code> | — | Scroll hide delay in ms, applicable only when type is set to `hover` or `scroll` |
| <code>scrollbarSize</code> | no | <code>string &#124; number</code> | — | Scrollbar size, any valid CSS value for width/height, numbers are converted to rem, default value is 12px (0.75rem) |
| <code>scrollbars</code> | no | <code>false &#124; "x" &#124; "y" &#124; "xy"</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>"x"</code>, <code>"xy"</code>, <code>"y"</code> | Axis at which scrollbars must be rendered - `'x'` - horizontal scrollbar only - `'y'` - vertical scrollbar only - `'xy'` - both scrollbars - `false` - no scrollbars rendered (content remains scrollable via mouse/touch) |
| <code>startScrollPosition</code> | no | <code>{ x?: number; y?: number; }</code> | — | Initial scroll position set on mount |
| <code>unstyled</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | — |
| <code>viewportProps</code> | no | <code>React.DetailedHTMLProps&lt;React.HTMLAttributes&lt;HTMLDivElement&gt;, HTMLDivElement&gt;</code> | — | Props passed down to the viewport element |

- Component-specific blocked props: none

#### Component-specific props

| Prop | Required | Type | Literal values | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>collapseThreshold</code> | no | <code>SplitterPaneSize</code> | — | Size below which the pane snaps to collapsed, defaults to `min` |
| <code>collapsible</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | Whether this pane can be collapsed |
| <code>defaultSize</code> | yes | <code>SplitterPaneSize</code> | — | Initial size, a `number`/`%` is a flexible size (shares leftover space), `px`/`rem` is a fixed size. A bare number is treated as a percentage. |

| <code>keepMounted</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | If set, all step content is kept mounted. React 19 `Activity` is used to preserve state while content is hidden. |
| <code>unstyled</code> | no | <code>false &#124; true</code> | <code>false</code>, <code>true</code> | — |
